# Literal tables for the central EFG capacity-one lemma

These are the complete fixed rational tables of [N7_CENTRAL_COMPLETION_EFG_CAP.md](N7_CENTRAL_COMPLETION_EFG_CAP.md). Each polynomial leaf prints its positive integer combination and every coefficient after the stated box substitution. Each scalar leaf prints its final contradictory bounds. There are no solver outputs or omitted source certificates in these tables.
